# Excalidraw Data
## Text Elements
ARBITRARY FUNCTION GENERATOR

 ^FLjQK3fb

"High Z" means it just outputs 

"Load X ohm" means there's a X ohm resistor connected in parallel
 ^gRBEUHM0

GREEDY ALGORITHM:

- Used when finding a specific resistance using resistors from the E-series

SERIES:
- The first resistor has to be greedy: takes up the most resistance needed.
- The next one has to also be greedy: takes up the most remaining resistance
- Keep going until the last one. If the last one isn't in the E-series, go backwards
- The prvious one goes down in greed, and the last one goes up.
- Repeat!

PARALLEL:
- The first resistor has to be greedy: takes up the most current, so a lower resistance, and just above needed.
- 1/(R_T) = 1/(R_1) + 1/(R_2) to find the next resistance needed.
- If that doesn't work, go back to the first resistor, increase greediness (next in the E-series). ^CNgeMDij

[[Year 1]] ^llmv6zHl
